<a id="51-doc50-eddy-jq-recompare-2026-09-23md"></a>

# File: `51_DOC50_EDDY_JQ_RECOMPARE_2026-09-23.md`

# Doc 51 — Doc-50 GetDP source: eddy J/Q re-compare vs SPH

Date: 2026-09-23  
Parents: [`50_GETDP_HOLE_JS_ROOT_CAUSE_2026-09-23.md`](50_GETDP_HOLE_JS_ROOT_CAUSE_2026-09-23.md), Doc 43 R05/R07  
Artifacts under `docs/ophelie/reference/em_rz_getdp/`:

- refreshed `reference_rz.csv` / `metadata.json` (conducting, I=1 A peak)
- `work/sph_rz_coil_only_dp02_doc50.csv`, `work/sph_rz_oneway_dp02_doc50.csv`
- `work/compare_aziavg_coil_only_doc50.json`, `work/compare_aziavg_oneway_doc50.json`
- pre-fix archive: `work/archive_pre_doc50_reference/`

## 1. Decision

**Coil-only axis |B| fix (Doc 50) stands.** Under the corrected GetDP mesh+`Js=I/A`, same-current eddy comparison vs SPH at I=1 A is:

| Observable | Pre–Doc 50 (historical) | Doc 50 source |
|---|---:|---:|
| `P_sph / P_getdp` (coil-only SPH) | **1.009** | **0.694** |
| Outer-shell ΔP (SPH vs GetDP probe) | ~0.5% | **~1.3%** |
| Midplane `corr_Q` (masked) | (see Doc 43) | **0.966** |
| Midplane `corr_Jθ` | | **0.980** |
| Full-glass masked `rel_L2_Q_abs` | **0.910** | **0.436** |

Absolute power no longer accidentally agrees. **Shape/shell agreement is better, not worse.** Do not restore `Js=2πI/A` or the broken `coil_loops` holes to recover P≈1. Prefer equal-power / shape gates for literature-adjacent work until the remaining ~30% absolute-P gap is apportioned (filament vs finite section, edge-flux vs MagDyn, dp).

Production stirring remains `--aind=off`. No skull/js0-thermal retune.

## 2. Signed-B convention (declare once)

| Source | Midplane coil-only `Bz_re` at r≈1e-4 |
|---|---|
| Independent / SPH filament Biot | **positive** (RH: +φ current → +z) |
| GetDP OnPoint `CompY[{d a}]` | **negative** |

Magnitudes now match filament to ~1% (coil-only Flag_coil_only). **Do not flip GetDP components to improve tables.** Future packs report signed components and `|B|` separately; acceptance uses `|B|` / power / shape unless an orientation bug is proven in the MagDyn basis.

## 3. What was regenerated

```bash
source ~/opt/onelab/env.sh
cd docs/ophelie/reference/em_rz_getdp
./run_reference.sh   # geo coil_loops[] + Js=I/A
# SPH:
test_3d_ophelie_french_aind_diagnostic ... --coil-current=1 --aind-one-way-feedback=0|1 --dump-rz-csv=...
python3 compare_sph_getdp.py --mode azi-avg --dp 0.02 ...
```

GetDP conducting metadata (authoritative power from J·2πr):

```text
P_absorbed_W (from J, 2πr) ≈ 2.129 W
P_OnGlobal JouleLosses × 2π ≈ 2.130 W
```

Pre–Doc 50 published GetDP power at the same I was ≈1.466 W (broken holes + 2π Js).

SPH coil-only edge-flux recon: `P_complex_coil_only ≈ 1.478 W` (unchanged; filament path).

## 4. Coil-only azi-avg summary (I=1 A, σ=16, dp=0.02)

| Metric | Value |
|---|---:|
| P_sph | 1.478 W |
| P_getdp (J, 2πr) | 2.129 W |
| P_sph/P_getdp | **0.694** |
| SPH P_outer frac | 0.695 |
| GetDP probe P_outer frac | 0.708 |
| ΔP_outer | 0.013 |
| Midplane corr_Q / corr_Jθ | 0.966 / 0.980 |
| Midplane rel_L2_Q_shape | 0.170 |
| Masked full-glass rel_L2_Q_abs | 0.436 |

One-way SPH (`P≈1.526 W`): `P_sph/P_getdp≈0.717`; midplane corr_Q≈0.964. Same qualitative story.

## 5. Interpretation

1. Doc 50 removed a **false** absolute-power agreement that mixed a geo bug with a compensating Js factor.
2. SPH filament excitation at I=1 A deposits ~30% less joule than corrected finite-section GetDP at the same I — consistent with a remaining **source-representation / solver-stack** gap, not with “SPH B wrong on axis” (axis coil-only B already closed).
3. Shell fractions and midplane radial shape remain the right gates for production `--aind=off` until absolute P is closed or an equal-power protocol is declared.

## 6. Claim table

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| GetDP reference regenerated under Doc 50 | **exercised** |
| Coil-only / one-way azi-avg vs new GetDP | **exercised** |
| Absolute P_sph≈P_getdp at I=1 | **withdrawn** (was 1.009; now ~0.69) |
| Midplane Q/J shape usable | **supported** (corr≳0.96) |
| Equal-power midplane shape (coil-only) | **exercised** (abs L2→shape L2) |
| Signed-B convention documented | **yes** |
| Absolute EM power identity closed | **not claimed** |
| Literature thermal baseline | **not claimed** |

## 7. Equal-power shape (controlled study)

Artifacts: `work/compare_aziavg_*_doc50_eqpower.json` (`--equal-power`: scale SPH Q by `P_getdp/P_sph`, `|J|` by `√scale`).

| Mode | scale_Q | midplane `rel_L2_Q_abs` → shape | midplane corr_Q |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Coil-only same-I | — | 0.377 → 0.170 | 0.966 |
| Coil-only equal-P | 1.440 | **0.172** → 0.170 | 0.966 |
| One-way same-I | — | 0.361 → 0.179 | 0.964 |
| One-way equal-P | 1.395 | **0.178** → 0.179 | 0.964 |

Under equal absorbed power, midplane absolute L2 collapses onto shape L2: the residual is **radial profile mismatch**, not an extra local amplitude bug. Shell fractions are unchanged by the scale (already ~1% ΔP_outer). Full-glass masked abs L2 stays ~0.46 — axial structure still differs.

**Do not** treat equal-power as closing absolute EM identity. Use it only for shape gates and literature-adjacent heating-pattern discussion.

## 8. Next

1. Apportion the ~30% absolute-P gap: finite-section vs filament excitation in SPH; edge-flux vs GetDP.
2. Doc 47 alongside thermal/ledger items (genuine F02 cadence; F03 freshness; F01/F05 production operators; F07 mass/ρ/V).
3. Defer 1000 s / Picard-on production / skull retune / paper contours.

# File: `52_F02_GENUINE_CADENCE_CLI_2026-09-23.md`

# Doc 52 — F02 genuine cadence: `--csv-every=` + protocol (GPU pending)

Date: 2026-09-23  
Parents: Doc 46 §3, Doc 47 §3 / §10.4, Doc 51  
Case: `test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_em`

## 1. Decision

Archived `batch_ab_cadence_compare.json` (screen 20 vs 100, `csv_every` stuck at 20) remains **withdrawn** as cadence control (`f02_cadence_ok=false`).

**Implemented:** CLI `--csv-every=N` (N=0 disables CSV). Startup logs `csv_every` and `screen_every`. Comparator script: `docs/ophelie/reference/stirring_stage3_r0/compare_f02_genuine_cadence.py`.

**Not yet exercised:** equal-horizon GPU pair with `csv_every==screen_every` differing between runs — this host has no usable NVIDIA driver. Do not set `f02_cadence_ok=true` until that pair exists.

## 2. Why the old twin failed

Shared energy/host diagnostic block runs under `write_csv || write_screen`. Changing only `--screen-every` leaves the block at `csv_every` cadence. Both archived energy CSVs have 30 data rows.

## 3. Protocol (when GPU is available)

```bash
BIN=build/tests/extra_source_and_tests/3d_examples/test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_em/bin
RELOAD=../../test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_glass_relax/bin/reload
COMMON="--reload-dir=$RELOAD --thermo-em-coupling=periodic --em-control=fixed-current --aind=off \
  --skull-resistance --skull-delta=0.005 --free-crust-resistance --free-crust-delta=0.005 \
  --constant-thermal-rheology --require-validation --end-time=2.0 \
  --em-update-interval=0.5 --boundary-area-normalize --no-state-recording --max-wall-hours=1"

cd $BIN
mkdir -p output_c20 output_c100
# run A / B with distinct OUTDIR (or rename after each)
./test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_em $COMMON --csv-every=20  --screen-every=20
./test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_em $COMMON --csv-every=100 --screen-every=100

python3 docs/ophelie/reference/stirring_stage3_r0/compare_f02_genuine_cadence.py \
  --a .../output_c20 --b .../output_c100 \
  --csv-every-a 20 --csv-every-b 100 \
  --out docs/ophelie/reference/stirring_stage3_r0/batch_ab_genuine_cadence_compare.json
```

Expect: energy CSV ~30 vs ~6 rows; ledger `|ΔE|/E` small under equal physics; `diagnostic_cadence_changed=true`.

Alternative: `--csv-every=0` and vary only screen (diagnostic cadence follows screen alone).

## 4. Claim table

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| `--csv-every=` CLI | **implemented** (+ rebuilt binary) |
| Cadence logged at startup | **implemented** |
| Genuine GPU cadence pair | **not exercised** (no GPU here) |
| `f02_cadence_ok=true` | **not claimed** |
| Old se20/se100 as cadence control | **withdrawn** (Doc 47) |

## 5. Next (alongside)

1. Run §3 on a GPU host; archive `batch_ab_genuine_cadence_compare.json`.
2. F03 schedule/freshness timing checks; F01/F05 production-operator thermal; F07 mass/ρ/V convention note.
3. Continue EM absolute-P apportionment (Doc 51) independently.

# File: `53_F03_F07_AND_EM_PGAP_2026-09-23.md`

# Doc 53 — Alongside Doc 47: F03 gate harden, F07 convention, EM P-gap note

Date: 2026-09-23  
Parents: Doc 47 §5/§7, Doc 51, Doc 52  
No GPU on this host → F02 genuine cadence twin and production thermal smokes remain deferred.

## 1. Decision

| Item | Status |
|---|---|
| F03 exclusive-endpoint refresh count (no blanket slack) | **implemented** + expanded negative tests |
| F03 absolute end-time tol (`1e-3` s, not 99% horizon) | **implemented** |
| F03 unmeasured transfer / out-of-grid cannot `validation_passed` | **implemented** |
| F03 last-refresh timestamp / max field age | **→ Doc 54** (nominal-tick + gate; GPU smoke pending) |
| F07 Mass vs ρ0·V vs ρ·V convention note | **documented** + audit fields extended |
| EM ~30% same-I absolute P gap apportionment | **bounded** (coil section ≠ cause) |
| F02 GPU cadence twin | **blocked** (no driver; Doc 52) |
| Literature thermal baseline | **not claimed** |

## 2. F03 gate changes

File: `electromagnetic_ophelie_thermo_em_coupling.h`

**Schedule.** `frenchExpectedPeriodicRefreshes` now counts `k` with `k·interval < end_time` (endpoint exclusive). Removed `kFrenchValidationRefreshScheduleSlack`.

| end_time | interval | expected (new) | note |
|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 2.0 | 0.5 | **3** | matches archived Batch A/B smoke |
| 30.0 | 0.5 | **59** | t=0.5…29.5 |
| 30.25 | 0.5 | **60** | missing one interior → fail |

**Horizon.** `completed_requested_time` requires `physical_time + 1e-3 ≥ end_time` (rejects former 99% false pass at 29.7/30).

**Measurement.** Periodic `validation_passed` requires `out_of_grid_measured`, `q_sample_rel_l2 ≥ 0`, and `fluid_power_recorded`. Unmeasured paths may still `smoke_passed` where previously allowed.

**Negative tests** now cover: under-refresh, missing interior (30.25/59), zero fluid heat, out-of-grid unmeasured, Q-L2 unmeasured, incomplete end time, sticky ledger invalid, baseline ok, end=2 exclusive=3.

Freshness timestamps / drift-bounded age remain OPEN (Doc 47 §5); do not claim schedule/freshness closed.

## 3. F07 mass / volume / density

### Framework convention (source)

- Init: `Mass = ρ₀ · Vol` (`BaseParticles::initializeBasicDiscreteVariables`).
- WCSPH update: `Vol = Mass / ρ` (`fluid_time_step_ck.h`).
- Therefore **Mass is the conserved particle mass**; VolumetricMeasure tracks compression.  
  `sum_mass ≠ ρ₀·ΣV` whenever mean density ≠ ρ₀ — **expected**, not mass loss.
- Material thermal energy still uses **`H = Σ ρ₀ cp V_i ΔT_i`** with heating `dΔT/dt = Q/(ρ₀ cp)`. When V evolves, ΔH includes a volume-change term (Doc 47 §7); that identity is not yet re-derived.

### Audit extension

`OphelieThermalMassVolumeAudit` now also reports `sum_rho_vol`, `rho_min/max/mean`. Stirring final line prints `mass_vs_rho0V_rel` and `mass_vs_rhoV_rel` (expect the latter ≈ 0).

Archived se20 gap `(sum_mass−sum_rho0_vol)/sum_mass ≈ 1.9%` is consistent with WCSPH density ≈ ρ₀×1.02 under c0=8 packing — **convention**, pending a GPU re-smoke to print `sum_rho_vol`.

**Do not** change Mass/Vol operators blindly. Before literature energy claims, either (a) document H_V as the engineering measure, or (b) switch diagnostics to mass-weighted enthalpy and re-qualify residuals.

## 4. EM absolute-P gap (Doc 51 follow-up)

Same-I coil-only: `P_sph/P_getdp ≈ 0.694`. Apportionment from existing evidence:

| Candidate | Evidence | Contribution to ~30% |
|---|---|---|
| 5 mm coil section vs filament | Batch C finite vs filament axis B ~ **10⁻⁵** rel | **negligible** |
| GetDP coil-only \|B\| vs filament after Doc 50 | ~1–2% → B² ~2–4% | **small** |
| Equal-power midplane | abs L2 → shape L2 | residual is **shape**, not hidden amp |
| Edge-flux recon / discrete SPH vs GetDP MagDyn | open | **dominant residual** |

**Claim:** absolute power identity at I=1 A remains **not closed**. Prefer shell/shape / equal-power gates for heating-pattern discussion. Next EM probe (when prioritized): same GetDP mesh with SPH-equivalent filament source in GetDP, or SPH finite-section Biot excitation — not skull/js0 retune.

## 5. Duplicate Doc 52

Removed stray `52_GENUINE_F02_CADENCE_CLI_2026-09-23.md`. Canonical: [`52_F02_GENUINE_CADENCE_CLI_2026-09-23.md`](52_F02_GENUINE_CADENCE_CLI_2026-09-23.md).

## 6. Rebuild / exercise

- Rebuilt `test_3d_ophelie_french_stirring_em` successfully after Doc 53 gate/audit changes.
- This host: no NVIDIA driver → in-process `gate_negative_tests_ok` not re-run here; expanded tests compile into the binary and will fire at next GPU startup.

## 7. Next

1. GPU: F02 genuine cadence (Doc 52) + confirm F07 `sum_rho_vol≈sum_mass` on smoke.
2. F03: optional refresh-time log / max-age field (still OPEN).
3. F01/F05 production-operator frozen thermal (Doc 47 §6).
4. Optional EM filament-in-GetDP or finite-section-in-SPH for absolute P.
5. Defer 1000 s / Picard-on / skull retune / paper T.

# File: `54_F03_FRESHNESS_AND_F01_PRODUCTION_SEQ_2026-09-23.md`

# Doc 54 — F03 freshness timestamps + F01/F05 production-sequence host check

Date: 2026-09-23  
Parents: Doc 47 §5–§6, Doc 53  
No GPU on this host → F02 genuine cadence twin still deferred.

## 1. Decision

| Item | Status |
|---|---|
| Nominal `next_due_time` refresh schedule (no `last+interval` drift) | **implemented** |
| Record max field age + nominal lateness; sticky refresh solver fails | **implemented** |
| Gate `freshness_ok` required for periodic `validation_passed` | **implemented** + negative tests |
| F01/F05 production order Q→Laplace→clamp (float32 ΔT) in Stage-1 host algebra | **implemented** |
| Spatial analytic MMS / particle production GPU thermal | **not claimed** |

## 2. F03 freshness

`ThermoEMCouplingState` now carries `next_due_time`, `max_field_age_s`, `max_nominal_lateness_s`, `freshness_measured`, `refresh_solver_fail_count`.

- After t=0 init: `armFrenchPeriodicRefreshSchedule(state, interval)` → first due at `interval`.
- Fire when `shouldUpdateEM(t, interval, next_due_time)`.
- On success: `noteFrenchPeriodicRefresh(...)` advances next-due (catch-up past `t` without inventing extra refreshes), updates age/lateness, sticky-counts bad φ/P.
- Gate: age and lateness ≤ `interval + 1e-3 s`; unmeasured freshness fails validation; sticky solver fail fails validation.

Wired in stirring and natural periodic paths; final line prints `freshness_ok` / ages.

## 3. F01/F05 host production sequence

Extended `verifyFrenchThermalStage1HostAlgebra` with the **same operator order** as stirring production (`ApplyGridJouleHeatToTemperatureCK` → `ApplyLaplaceDiffusionDtCK` → inactive/active `ClampTemperatureCK`), float32 ΔT-authoritative. Runs at stirring/natural/MMS startup.

Still **not** a multi-particle spatial MMS or GPU production-field proof (Doc 47 §6 remaining items 1–2).

| GPU freshness on 2 s smoke | **not exercised** here |
| Literature thermal baseline | **not claimed** |

2. Optional: small spatial MMS with production operators (Doc 47 §6.2).

# Doc 56 — EM P-gap midplane J scale + corrected 1-rev GPU

Date: 2026-09-23

## 2. EM absolute-P apportionment (update)

Same-I coil-only @ σ=16, Doc 50 GetDP:

| Factor | Effect on P_sph/P_getdp |
|---|---|
| `vol_sph/vol_nominal` ≈ 0.954 | ~−5% only |

| `√(P_sph/P_getdp)` | **≈0.833** |

Amplitude ratio matches √P within ~2%: residual is consistent with a **global scale** between SPH edge-flux recon and GetDP MagDyn, not a midplane shape bug (equal-power midplane L2 already → shape L2).

Driving-field hint: midplane `|Bz_coil|_SPH / |Bz|_GetDP` ≈ 0.81 (GetDP field is conducting total B — qualitative only). Next EM probe if needed: GetDP Flag_coil_only glass |B| vs SPH `Bz_coil` on the same probes; optional thinner GetDP coil. **Do not** restore `Js=2πI/A`.

Artifact: `reference/em_rz_getdp/work/doc56_em_pgap_midplane_jt.json`.

--skull-resistance/--free-crust δ=5 mm --boundary-area-normalize

<a id="57-coil-only-b-drive-vs-p-gap-2026-09-24md"></a>

# File: `57_COIL_ONLY_B_DRIVE_VS_P_GAP_2026-09-24.md`

# Doc 57 — Coil-only |B|: SPH `Bz_coil` vs GetDP Flag_coil_only (resume)

Date: 2026-09-24  
Parents: Doc 51/56 (P-gap), Doc 50 (Js+holes)  
Note: Prior attempt on 2026-09-23 was **interrupted** after writing `compare_coil_b_sph_getdp.py`; this doc completes that work.

## 1. Decision

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| GetDP Flag_coil_only on published baseline mesh | **exercised** |
| Common-support SPH filament Biot vs GetDP coil-only \|B\| | **exercised** |
| Same-I P gap (~0.69) largely tracks **drive-\|B\|²** in glass | **supported** |
| Eddy recon as primary residual after drive B matches | **not supported** (secondary) |
| Absolute EM identity closed | **not claimed** |

## 2. Setup

- GetDP: same `french_cylinder_axi.geo` defaults as published eddy; `Flag_coil_only=1`; Doc 50 `Js=I/A`.
- Probes: 525 sites from published `reference_rz.csv`.
- SPH: `work/sph_rz_coil_only_dp02_doc50.csv` columns `Br_coil_*` / `Bz_coil_*` (filament Biot).
- Script: `compare_coil_b_sph_getdp.py` → `work/doc57_coil_b_common_support.json`.
- Artifacts: `work/coil_only_baseline_doc57/` (mesh, pos, CSV, metadata).

Axis check (OnPoint): GetDP \|Bz\|=1.409e-5 T vs filament 1.433e-5 (**rel_err≈1.6%**). P_absorbed under coil-only ≈0 (as expected).

## 3. Common-support ratios (SPH coil / GetDP coil-only)

| Region | mean \|B\| ratio | median |
|---|---:|---:|
| All 525 probes | **0.862** | 0.858 |
| Midplane, r≥0.05 (n=60) | **0.840** | 0.848 |
| All probes \|Bz\| | 0.846 | 0.829 |

\|Br\| ratios are unstable near nodes (ignore for acceptance).

## 4. Link to Doc 56 P-gap

| Observable | Value |
|---|---:|
| Midplane \|B\|_drive ratio (this doc) | ≈0.84 |
| Midplane \|Jθ\|_sph/\|Jθ\|_getdp (Doc 56, conducting) | ≈0.85 |
| √(P_sph/P_getdp) | ≈0.83 |
| (0.84)² | ≈**0.70** ≈ P ratio **0.694** |

**Interpretation:** At the same I=1 A, SPH filament drive \|B\| inside the glass is ~16% below GetDP’s finite-section coil-only MagDyn field on the same probes. Because Q∝|J|²∝|E|² and the induced field scales with drive B, that alone accounts for essentially the whole same-I power gap. Axis agreement with filament remains good for both solvers; the mismatch is **off-axis / in-melt excitation representation** (filament vs 5 mm section + MagDyn), not a broken eddy Joule formula after Doc 50.

Production `--aind=off` inherits this drive. Prefer shell/shape / equal-power gates until a controlled filament-source GetDP or finite-section SPH Biot is exercised.

## 5. Claim table

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| Coil-only baseline solve under Doc 50 | **exercised** |
| SPH vs GetDP coil-only glass \|B\| | **exercised** (mean mid≈0.84) |
| P-gap ≈ (drive \|B\| ratio)² | **supported** |
| Need eddy-stack retune to close P | **not indicated** |
| Literature thermal baseline | **not claimed** |

## 6. Next

1. Optional controlled study: GetDP filament-like thin coil, or SPH finite-section Biot — expect P ratio → 1 without touching js0/skull.
2. 5-rev only if a transient question appears; else keep 1-rev (Doc 56) as the contract short run.
3. Defer 1000 s / Picard-on / skull retune / paper T.

<a id="58-getdp-thin-coil-control-2026-09-24md"></a>

# File: `58_GETDP_THIN_COIL_CONTROL_2026-09-24.md`

# Doc 58 — GetDP thin-coil control vs SPH filament (P-gap cause)

Date: 2026-09-24  
Parents: Doc 51 (P≈0.694), Doc 57 (drive |B| mid≈0.84 → (0.84)²≈P gap)  
Hypothesis under test: same-I absolute gap closes if GetDP coil section → filament-like.

## 1. Decision

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| Geo `coil_a`/`coil_b` overridable via `-setnumber` | **done** (mesh extents verified) |
| Thin-coil ladder: half = 2.5 / 1.0 / 0.5 / 0.25 mm | **exercised** |
| Midplane drive \|B\| ratio SPH/GetDP → 1 as section → 0 | **falsified** (stays ≈0.840) |
| P_sph/P_getdp → 1 as section → 0 | **falsified** (stays ≈0.694) |
| Doc 57 numerics (B² ↔ P) | **still hold** as correlation |
| Doc 57 causal story “5 mm section vs filament” | **not supported** |

**Do not** retune js0 / skull / restore `Js=2πI/A`. Production stays `--aind=off` with equal-power / shape gates.

## 2. Setup

- GetDP: `french_cylinder_axi.geo` + `french_cylinder_eddy.pro` (Doc 50 `Js=I/A`, `coil_loops[]`).
- Override: `-setnumber coil_a=coil_b=HALF`, `lc_c` matched to section; **does not** overwrite published `reference_rz.csv`.
- SPH (fixed): `work/sph_rz_coil_only_dp02_doc50.csv` filament Biot + edge-flux recon, I=1 A.
- Scripts: `run_doc58_thin_coil.sh`, `compare_coil_b_sph_getdp.py`, `compare_sph_getdp.py`.
- Artifacts: `work/doc58_thin_a{HALF}/` (gmsh/getdp logs, coil_only + eddy CSV/meta, `summary.json`).

Mesh sanity (coil physical surfaces 10–16 node bbox):

| half [m] | full section | coil Δr [m] | nodes (mesh) |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 0.0025 | 5 mm | 0.00500 | 4872 |
| 0.001 | 2 mm | 0.00200 | 7020 |
| 0.0005 | 1 mm | 0.00100 | 7569 |
| 0.00025 | 0.5 mm | 0.00050 | 9644 |

## 3. Results (summary.json)

| half [mm full] | mid \|B\| ratio | all \|B\| ratio | P_sph/P_getdp | mid corr_Q | axis \|Bz\|_getdp [T] |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 5.0 (baseline) | **0.8396** | 0.8622 | **0.6944** | 0.9661 | 1.4094e-5 |
| 2.0 | 0.8396 | 0.8622 | 0.6943 | 0.9661 | 1.4094e-5 |
| 1.0 | 0.8396 | 0.8622 | 0.6943 | 0.9661 | 1.4094e-5 |
| 0.5 | 0.8395 | 0.8622 | 0.6943 | 0.9661 | 1.4095e-5 |

Baseline row reproduces Doc 57 / Doc 51 within rounding. Thinning by **10×** moves neither drive-B nor absolute-P ratios at the reported precision.

## 4. Interpretation

1. Inside the glass (R≤0.25, coil at R_c=0.285), a loop’s B is almost insensitive to turn cross-section once a ≪ R_c. Shrinking GetDP rectangles therefore cannot close a ~16% midplane \|B\| gap that already exists against SPH filaments at the same I and R_c.
2. The Doc 57 identity “(mid drive \|B\| ratio)² ≈ P ratio” remains a useful **diagnostic correlation**, but the residual is **not** “finite 5 mm copper vs ideal filament.”
3. Remaining candidates (for discussion, not yet exercised here):
   - MagDyn A–V (axi) vs SPH discrete Biot loop stack (formulation / gauge / continuum vs 7 wires).
   - SPH edge-flux recon vs GetDP MagDyn eddy path (secondary for absolute P once drive B mismatches).
   - SPH `Bz_coil` sampling / dp=0.02 azi-avg vs GetDP probe grid.
   - Optional: SPH **finite-section** Biot (opposite control) — expect little change if (1) is right.

## 5. Claim table

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| Thin GetDP coil control completed | **exercised** |
| Section size is the dominant absolute-P cause | **not supported** |
| Keep shape / equal-power gates | **affirmed** |
| Absolute EM identity closed | **not claimed** |

## 6. Next

1. **Discussion pack** (this is a decision fork): MagDyn-vs-Biot vs recon vs sampling.
2. Defer 5-rev / 1000 s / Picard-on / skull / paper T.
3. Optional later: SPH finite-section Biot smoke (expect null if Doc 58 stands); or single-loop MagDyn vs Biot analytic.

<a id="59-gpt-pack-thin-coil-falsification-2026-09-24md"></a>

# File: `59_GPT_PACK_THIN_COIL_FALSIFICATION_2026-09-24.md`

# ChatGPT discussion pack — Doc 58 thin-coil falsifies “section vs filament” P-gap story

Date: 2026-09-24  
Paste this file (+ attached tarball contents) to discuss the next EM move.  
Production constraint unchanged: `--aind=off`; no js0/skull/`Js=2πI/A` restore; no 1000 s / Picard-on / paper-T campaign.

## 0. Ask of ChatGPT

We closed a controlled GetDP study that was supposed to confirm Doc 57’s causal claim. It **falsified** the cause while **preserving** the numeric correlation. We need a ranked next probe — not a thermal retune.

**Questions:**

1. Given thin-coil GetDP (section 5→0.5 mm) leaves midplane drive `|B|_SPH/|B|_GetDP≈0.84` and `P_sph/P_getdp≈0.694` unchanged, what is the most likely remaining cause of the glass midplane drive-B gap at fixed I and R_c=0.285?
2. Should we next (a) analytic/single-loop MagDyn vs Biot, (b) SPH finite-section Biot (expect null?), (c) treat absolute P as permanently equal-power-gated and stop EM absolute work, or (d) something else?
3. Does “(drive |B| ratio)² ≈ P ratio” still justify calling the residual a **drive** problem rather than an eddy-recon problem, even after section is ruled out?

## 1. Prior chain (compressed)

| Doc | Result |
|---|---|
| 50 | GetDP holes + `Js=I/A` fixed; axis coil-only \|B\| ≈ filament |
| 51 | Same-I conducting: `P_sph/P_getdp≈0.694`; midplane shape/shell good (corr_Q≈0.966) |
| 56/57 | Coil-only glass midplane \|B\| ratio ≈0.84; (0.84)²≈0.70≈P gap → “filament vs 5 mm section” story |
| **58** | Thin GetDP coil ladder: ratio **stuck** at 0.84 / 0.694 |

Signed-B note (Doc 51): GetDP OnPoint `Bz` sign opposite SPH; acceptance uses `|B|`.

## 2. Doc 58 evidence (authoritative)

Mesh coil Δr really shrank: 5.0 → 2.0 → 1.0 → 0.5 mm (physical surfaces 10–16).  
Axis GetDP `|Bz|` stays ≈1.409e-5 T (vs SPH filament ≈1.433e-5, ~1.6%).  
Glass midplane `|B|` ratio and P ratio do **not** move (see Doc 58 table).

Artifacts: `docs/ophelie/reference/em_rz_getdp/work/doc58_thin_a*/summary.json`.

## 3. What we are not asking

- Do not propose restoring broken `Js=2πI/A` or pre-Doc50 holes to recover P≈1.
- Do not propose skull/js0 thermal retune or paper-contour fitting.
- Do not require 1000 s / Picard-on / 5-rev as the next step.

## 4. Preferred answer shape

- Ranked hypotheses with a **single next experiment** (cheap, falsifiable).
- Explicit: keep or drop equal-power gating for literature-adjacent thermal work.
- If MagDyn vs Biot is primary, sketch the smallest axi test that separates formulation from SPH packing/dp.

<!-- ===== BEGIN 60_POST_DOC58_EM_SHAPE_REVIEW_AND_DECISIVE_PROBE_2026-09-24.md ===== -->

<a id="60-post-doc58-em-shape-review-and-decisive-probe-2026-09-24md"></a>

# File: `60_POST_DOC58_EM_SHAPE_REVIEW_AND_DECISIVE_PROBE_2026-09-24.md`

# Doc 60 — Post-Doc58 review: EM shape gap and the next decisive measurement

Date: 2026-09-24  
Input: `gpt_doc58_thin_coil_2026-09-24.tar.gz`  
Predecessor review: Doc 47  
Audience: Cursor and the project owner

## 1. Decision

**Do not classify the remaining discrepancy as a fundamental SPH-versus-MagDyn limitation, and do not permanently waive same-current EM validation.**

Doc 50 identifies an important reference-model correction. Doc 58 provides a useful negative control: shrinking the coil section by a factor of ten does not remove the observed discrepancy. Keep these advances. Stop investigating coil section as the primary explanation for this gap.

The next experiment should be an **off-axis, same-coordinate, source-only A/B comparison against an independent seven-loop reference**, using the existing configuration. Compare direct solver evaluations before averaging, and check exported samples against the same evaluations. This separates runtime source identity, coordinate/data association, FEM output/extraction, and spatial sampling. It is cheaper and more decisive than another thermal run or a new finite-section SPH implementation.

The current evidence does **not** establish that the whole remaining power error is caused by excitation. It establishes an unresolved coil-field comparison discrepancy, a same-current power discrepancy, and a substantial full-domain heat-source shape discrepancy. These may share a cause, but that causal link has not been demonstrated.

## 2. Review scope: what was actually provided

The archive contains 23 files: Docs 50/51/56/57/58/59; Gmsh geometry and GetDP model; a thin-coil runner; three Python comparison/export scripts; and selected JSON summaries/metadata.

It does not contain:

- Docs 48/49/52–55 and their underlying regression evidence;
- current SPH C++ source, runtime coil parameter dump or diagnostic run command;
- the SPH CSV used by the new comparisons;
- raw new B/J `.pos` files, GetDP meshes, solution files or solver logs;
- the included `Lib_Magnetodynamics2D_av_Cir.pro` or current `parse_getdp_table.py`;
- corrected 1-revolution raw thermal outputs.

Therefore:

- This review directly checks the supplied comparison/export/model code and arithmetic consistency of the JSON results.
- Doc 50 isolation experiments and Doc 56's corrected thermal run are **reported evidence**, not independently rerun/fully audited here.
- Older SPH source is useful historical context, but is not substituted for the missing current executable/source identity.
- I independently computed the declared ideal seven-loop source and its prescribed-field heating benchmark; that is not a rerun of SPH or GetDP.

## 3. Progress since Doc 47

### 3.1 Reference source repair is a real advance

Doc 50 describes two coupled reference-model errors:

1. The air-surface hole list needed `coil_loops[]`.
2. The old `2*pi*I/A` excitation had compensated for the geometry error; the corrected source is `I/A`.

The supplied `.geo` and `.pro` contain these fixes. The reported single-loop and turn-count controls are a reasonable way to distinguish a source normalization mistake from a solver defect. This is a justified model correction, not an invitation to tune the source until SPH power agrees.

Do not restore the old geometry/current convention. Preserve the pre-fix results as superseded historical evidence.

### 3.2 Thin-coil study rules against the finite-section explanation

From the supplied JSONs:

| Full coil section [mm] | Midplane mean B ratio | P_SPH/P_GetDP | P_GetDP [W] |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 5.0 | 0.8395858 | 0.6943567 | 2.1289211 |
| 2.0 | 0.8395644 | 0.6943457 | 2.1289550 |
| 1.0 | 0.8395594 | 0.6943405 | 2.1289708 |
| 0.5 | 0.8395283 | 0.6942861 | 2.1291378 |

The controls do not support a coil-section explanation for a roughly 16% B or 31% P discrepancy. A local coil-mesh change is included in the ladder, so these are not a rigorous isolated cross-section convergence proof, but the almost unchanged result is useful evidence against section size as the dominant cause. Raw geometry extents are reported in Doc 58 rather than independently available in this pack.

Do not spend the next cycle building finite-section SPH solely to explain this discrepancy.

### 3.3 Thermal progress should be retained at its documented scope

Doc 56 reports a corrected 6 s/one-revolution run with throughput residual about 0.0596%, net-storage residual about 0.477%, and Mass matching sum(rho*V). This is encouraging engineering progress. Without its raw data and updated code, this review neither re-certifies nor rejects it.

`Mass = rho*V` helps explain why `Mass != rho0*V` can occur. That identity alone does not establish that every thermal source/storage/volume update uses a consistent conserved measure. Keep the distinction from Doc 47.

## 4. Confirmed problems and overstatements in the new evidence chain

### 4.1 “Common support” is still not the same measurement operator

File: `compare_coil_b_sph_getdp.py`, loop over probes.

- GetDP: choose the nearest triangle centroid from `b.pos` and use its averaged nodal vector.
- SPH: average complex cylindrical components from all particles inside a radius-0.025 m disk in (r,z), then compute the magnitude.
- Empty SPH disks silently switch to nearest-particle sampling, although the supplied result reports zero such fallbacks.

Thus the same nominal probe label combines a point-like FEM sample with a spatially smoothed SPH field. The J/Q comparison has the same asymmetry. In the J/Q JSON, each SPH sample averages about 171 particles on average.

This is acceptable as an exploratory profile comparison, but it cannot cleanly identify a 16% source-field defect. The code does not retain the actual selected FEM centroid, its distance to the requested point, or the SPH weighted sample center/spread.

**Required distinction:** direct point agreement, exporter agreement and identically filtered-field agreement are three separate checks. Do not infer one from another.

Do not assume smoothing is the entire explanation either. Its effect must be measured with the independent field evaluated at the actual input positions.

### 4.2 The data reject a blanket “global amplitude only” description

Baseline JSON `compare_aziavg_coil_only_sph.json` reports:

| Metric | Value |
|---|---:|
| Masked full-domain absolute Q L2 | 0.435930 |
| Masked full-domain normalized-shape Q L2 | 0.426135 |
| Masked full-domain Q correlation | 0.753049 |
| Midplane normalized-shape Q L2 | 0.170490 |
| Midplane Q correlation | 0.966137 |

Normalizing amplitude barely changes the full-domain L2. Therefore there is a substantial shape mismatch in the current comparison, not merely one missing scale factor.

Likewise, the all-probe B ratios range approximately **0.433–1.240**, with p10/p90 approximately **0.643/1.122**. Even the midplane subset spans approximately 0.766–0.899. A single arithmetic mean of these ratios is not a global amplitude fit.

High midplane correlation supports a similar radial trend in that slice. It does not validate the axial structure, the full heating pattern or the temperature field.

The comparison operators themselves remain suspect, so these numbers do not yet prove a continuum solver shape error. Correct the comparison before assigning the remaining discrepancy to either solver.

### 4.3 Shell agreement depends on which FEM measure is used

The same JSON reports:

- SPH outer-shell power fraction: **0.695139**.
- FEM probe estimate: **0.707718**, difference **0.012579**.
- FEM triangle-integral estimate: **0.726038**, difference **0.030899**.

These are approximately 1.26 versus 3.09 **percentage points**, not interchangeable measures. The probe grid is built from the range of triangle centroids, then sampled inside that range. It does not cover the full physical cylinder up to every boundary. Its shell fraction is consequently not a substitute for the element-integrated fraction.

Report both, prioritizing a consistently integrated, common physical-domain comparison for energy fractions. A favorable probe fraction cannot close the full-field shape question.

### 4.4 Export metadata still contradicts the model and runner

File: `export_reference_csv.py`, metadata construction. The supplied thin-coil metadata repeats these discrepancies:

| Metadata field | Exported value | Model/runner value |
|---|---|---|
| Source convention | `2*pi*I_peak/Area` | `.pro`: `I_peak/AreaCoil` |
| Coil section | 5 mm × 5 mm in every case | Thin case is 0.5 mm × 0.5 mm |
| Outer radius | 1.0 m | `.geo` default used by runner: 1.5 m |
| z padding | 0.4 m | `.geo`: 0.6 m |
| Glass mesh size | 0.020 m | `.geo`: 0.010 m |
| Air mesh size | 0.060 m | `.geo`: 0.050 m |
| Mesh file | sibling `mesh.msh` | runner uses `french_cylinder_axi.msh`; eddy directory does not receive that mesh |

The export CLI gets only `--lc-coil`, not the other actual mesh/domain parameters. Its fixed physics block also does not distinguish effective sigma=1e-12 in source-only runs from the conducting sigma setting.

These are confirmed provenance errors. They do not prove that the solver used the wrong geometry, but they prevent reliable source identity and reproducibility claims.

Generate metadata from the actual run configuration, record source-only/conducting mode, effective conductivity, all coil centers and applied current, and include hashes/identities of the mesh, solution, CSV and SPH binary. Do not repair JSON by hand while leaving the exporter stale.

### 4.5 The forbidden component substitution survives in another exporter path

`export_reference_csv.py::build_axis_bz_metadata` still does:

```python
if getdp_bz < 0.2 * filament_bz and axis["Br_abs_T"] > 0.5 * filament_bz:
    getdp_bz = axis["Br_abs_T"]
```

This repeats the Doc 47 concern: an unexpected component may be replaced by another component because it looks more plausible. Remove it. Report a mismatch or format error. There is no evidence that this branch triggered in the supplied axis results, so do not attribute the current 0.84 ratio to it.

### 4.6 Runner does not preserve all evidence it computes

`run_doc58_thin_coil.sh` invokes `PowerAuditPos`, but the per-case eddy copy omits `P_2piR.txt`, `P_AxiSqu.txt`, `q_glass.pos`, `az.pos` and `b_r20.txt`. The next case removes shared `work/*.pos` and `work/*.txt` files. Important cross-checks can therefore be lost.

It also suppresses required copy failures with `2>/dev/null || true`, reuses result directories, and writes through shared working paths. This creates a stale-output/concurrent-run risk; it is not proof that the current runs were stale.

Use isolated run directories or enforce serial execution. Fail on missing required files, archive the independent power checks and A output, and verify file identities before comparing.

## 5. Why the B-squared correlation does not close causality

In a regular axisymmetric cylinder with no imposed azimuthal voltage, uniform scalar conductivity, peak phasors with exp(+i*omega*t), and a prescribed coil field:

```text
Phi(r,z) = 2*pi * integral_0^r Bz(r',z) * r' dr'
Aphi(r,z) = Phi(r,z)/(2*pi*r)
Ephi = -i*omega*Aphi
Jphi = sigma*Ephi
Q = 0.5*sigma*omega^2*|Aphi|^2
```

Thus the relevant excitation for local circumferential heating is magnetic flux/Aphi (and ultimately E), not the local magnitude |B| at that point. The general A–phi solver also contains the scalar-potential gradient; it cannot be dismissed without checking the applicable symmetry and discretization.

If an entire excitation field scales by one constant with all other conditions fixed, its linear response scales and power scales quadratically. But

```text
(square of an unweighted mean of local |B| ratios)
```

is not an identity for

```text
(ratio of volume-integrated Joule powers).
```

Here the field ratios vary, sampling operators differ, and SPH coil-only reconstruction is being compared with a conducting/self-consistent GetDP solution. The numerical similarity 0.84²≈0.694 is a useful hypothesis, not evidence that excludes reconstruction, scalar-potential correction, domain support or feedback effects.

**Amend Docs 56/57:** retain the numerical correlation; withdraw claims that it establishes essentially the entire cause or rules the eddy/reconstruction path secondary before the appropriate A/E/J tests.

Also, a gauge change by itself cannot change physical B or consistent E/J. “Different gauges” is not a physical explanation for a persistent B discrepancy. Axisymmetric FEM and Biot–Savart should converge to the same source-only physical field for matched sources, material and boundary conditions. A finite-domain boundary or discretization can differ; that is a testable numerical/model difference.

## 6. Independent numerical reference computed in this review

I evaluated the actual line integrals directly in double precision, without SPH or GetDP:

```text
mu0 = 4*pi*1e-7 H/m
I = 1 A peak per turn
Rcoil = 0.285 m
z_k = -0.01 + (k+0.5)*0.23/7, k=0..6
Glass: 0<=r<=0.25 m, 0<=z<=0.21 m
sigma = 16 S/m; f = 300 kHz
Free space, no magnetic feedback from induced current
```

Loop-center heights [m]:

```text
0.0064285714, 0.0392857143, 0.0721428571, 0.105,
0.1378571429, 0.1707142857, 0.2035714286
```

### 6.1 Exact-coordinate field targets for the declared source

At z=0.105 m, symmetry gives Br≈0:

| r [m] | Aphi [Wb/m] | Bz [T] |
|---:|---:|---:|
| 0.0001 | 7.1644367e-10 | 1.4328874e-5 |
| 0.05 | 3.6132516e-7 | 1.4578533e-5 |
| 0.10 | 7.4212556e-7 | 1.5379465e-5 |
| 0.15 | 1.1664206e-6 | 1.6901024e-5 |
| 0.20 | 1.6680939e-6 | 1.9441208e-5 |
| 0.24 | 2.1561345e-6 | 2.2396156e-5 |

At r=0.24 m, z=0.02/0.19 m:

```text
Aphi = 1.8420025e-6 Wb/m
Bz   = 1.8689519e-5 T
Br   = -/+7.5708992e-6 T
```

A separate 4×4 Gauss average over a uniform 5 mm square coil section changes the B vector at these selected probes by roughly **0.0011%–0.0023%**. These are selected-point results, not a global error bound. They independently support abandoning the 16% finite-section explanation.

### 6.2 A prescribed-field heating benchmark

For this axisymmetric no-feedback model, integrating `0.5*sigma*omega²*Aphi²` over the full cylinder gives:

```text
P0 = 2.275390477 W at I_peak=1 A per turn
```

Quadrature checks:

| r/z Gauss order | Angular samples per loop | P0 [W] |
|---:|---:|---:|
| 24×24 | 512 | 2.275390476897 |
| 48×48 | 1024 | 2.275390477015 |
| 64×64 | 2048 | 2.275390477015 |

An independent elliptic-integral expression for Aphi reproduces the value to floating-point precision. These digits describe numerical integration of the stated ideal model, not experimental or literature accuracy.

Interpretation alongside the supplied results:

- Archived SPH coil-only reconstructed power: 1.478230675 W, about **0.64966 P0**.
- Archived conducting GetDP power: 2.128921116 W, about **0.93563 P0**.

**Do not demand that the conducting GetDP value equal P0.** It includes induced-field feedback and finite-domain/discretization effects. Likewise, the SPH sum covers a discrete support that is only about 95.38% of nominal volume. These are not identical experiments.

P0 supplies a new independent no-feedback target. The cheap decisive intermediate is the analytic heating integrated over the **actual SPH particle positions/volumes**, then comparison with stored A and reconstructed J/Q on those same particles. That separates support quadrature from field/reconstruction error without a production Picard solve.

## 7. Ranked hypotheses

This ranking is diagnostic priority, not a claimed probability or proven root cause.

1. **Measurement/source identity and exported-data association.** Axis agreement reported in Docs 48/50 and strong spatially varying discrepancies in averaged old SPH dumps must be reconciled. Check actual coil settings, current, source softening, coordinates/origin/axis, direct versus stored fields, particle IDs/order, build/CSV identity and field scaling/restoration. Missing raw SPH data currently prevents this check.
2. **Mismatched sampling or FEM postprocessing.** Direct OnPoint versus parsed `.pos` versus nearest centroid must be compared at known points. Apply the same averaging to the independent field as to each solver before interpreting smoothed profiles.
3. **FEM spatial discretization, domain boundary, geometry/region coverage or physical-field conversion.** If exact-coordinate FEM values disagree with the independent source, isolate these next. Correct holes and an axis check do not prove the entire off-axis field.
4. **SPH no-feedback A–phi/edge-flux reconstruction and boundary/support effects.** Keep this open for the power/Q gap even if a B discrepancy is found. B is not the numerical input sufficient to certify E/J reconstruction.
5. **Induced feedback mismatch between the two comparison modes.** Check through a matched prescribed-field/weak-coupling limit before claiming a self-consistent same-current identity. One SPH feedback iteration changing P by ~3% is not a general error bound.

Low priority: finite 5 mm section, a supposed inherent continuum-versus-seven-wires difference, or gauge as a physical B explanation.

Doc 56's “volume only ~5%” also needs qualification: missing volume fraction is not missing heating fraction when Q is strongest near the wall. Evaluate the independent Q on the actual support. A 4.6% volume deficit alone cannot bound the power contribution to 4.6%.

## 8. One next experiment: direct A/B triangulation with data-path controls

### 8.1 Keep the existing seven-loop problem

Use the source-only GetDP mode and SPH coil evaluator at I=1 A, with the declared geometry above. No current rescaling, no thermal changes, no induced-current feedback for this field comparison.

Probe the six midplane points in §6.1 and the two off-midplane points at r=.24, z=.02/.19. This is one small probe experiment, not a new convergence campaign.

For every probe record:

- requested physical xyz and the explicit FEM r–z coordinate transform;
- independent Aphi, Br and Bz;
- the **actual SPH production coil routine** evaluated directly at that xyz;
- GetDP OnPoint physical Aphi, Br and Bz;
- separately, the existing `.pos` parsing/nearest-centroid result plus the actual centroid and distance.

Use the same runtime source parameters for the independent integration. Record segment count and softening; compare the intended filament limit and the actual softened/discretized operator separately if needed.

For A, inspect the installed GetDP template and verify whether the exported variable is physical Aphi or a transformed/flux quantity. Do not guess an r or 2*pi correction. A useful check is the regular-axis flux identity `2*pi*r*Aphi = integral_disk Bz dS`.

Determine coordinate/winding orientation geometrically and document the transform once. Retain raw signed complex values; do not select signs or components to minimize error. At symmetry zeros use absolute errors scaled to a representative B, not relative errors of Br≈0.

### 8.2 Audit the archived SPH field at its own positions

In the same diagnostic/export task, dump actual particle xyz, stable particle ID, volume, stored Acoil/Bcoil and fresh direct Acoil/Bcoil at that particle coordinate. Compare both against the independent source. This catches a correct point-evaluator test accompanied by a stale/misaligned production field dump.

If sorting/reloading occurred, explicitly check association before and after it. This is a hypothesis to test, not an accusation that sorting is currently broken.

### 8.3 Report a power decomposition on the actual SPH support

At sigma=16 and 300 kHz, with current held fixed:

```text
P0_continuum = full-cylinder integral of independent prescribed-coil Q
P0_particles = sum_i [0.5*sigma*omega²*|Aphi_independent(x_i)|² * V_i]
P_stored_A   = same sum using the physical stored coil Aphi
P_recon     = sum_i Q_reconstructed_i * V_i
```

Also report non-azimuthal A/J components and scalar-potential correction norms when assessing the symmetric no-feedback limit. Do not silently replace general 3D reconstructed Q with Jtheta-only Q.

The differences isolate domain quadrature, runtime field identity, and reconstruction respectively. This is much more informative than comparing local mean |B| ratios with sqrt(total power ratio).

### 8.4 Decision branches

| Result | Next action |
|---|---|
| Direct A/B agree on both sides, archived/averaged comparison fails | Fix export/data association/filtering; regenerate B and Q comparisons. |
| SPH direct evaluator fails its matched-source reference | Inspect runtime settings, source discretization/softening and coil function before any eddy changes. |
| SPH direct passes, stored field fails | Inspect field assignment, particle IDs/order, coordinate conversion and scaling/restoration. |
| FEM OnPoint fails, SPH direct passes | Use a single-loop off-axis analytic test and a controlled glass/air mesh or boundary variation; inspect actual template and source region coverage. |
| A/B pass; P0_particles differs greatly from P0_continuum | Resolve support/quadrature and boundary contribution. |
| P_stored_A matches P0_particles but reconstructed P/Q does not | Investigate phi correction, edge flux/reconstruction and dp/boundary consistency. |
| No-feedback path passes; conducting comparison fails | Investigate matched self-consistent feedback and complex E/J, not coil thickness. |

A few-percent FEM discrepancy may require discretization/domain assessment. Do not impose a universal 1% threshold before establishing those errors, and do not move a threshold just to admit the current 16% discrepancy.

## 9. Equal-power policy and literature work

**Keep equal-power normalization as an explicitly labeled diagnostic and, where the paper prescribes absorbed power, as part of the eventual physical operating-point protocol. Do not use it to certify an unresolved source/reconstruction model.**

At present:

- Same-current validation remains OPEN.
- Full-domain Q shape validation remains OPEN; current shape L2≈0.426 is too large to describe as a completed heating-pattern reproduction.
- Midplane correlation and shell profiles are useful partial evidence.
- Thermal infrastructure tests can proceed with a declared provisional Q field if needed, but must not be presented as validated literature temperature predictions.
- Production `--aind=off` can remain the operational baseline while its approximation is quantified. It is not an automatic statement of physical completeness.

Do not permanently abandon absolute-power work: the newly available independent no-feedback reference provides a cheap way to localize the gap. Do not spend the next cycle on long thermal runs, skull fitting or full-production N² induced-field feedback.

## 10. Concrete Cursor task list

1. Correct Doc 56/57 causal wording and full-domain versus midplane scope.
2. Repair metadata generation, remove the surviving component substitution, and archive all mandatory probe/power outputs.
3. Run the eight-point direct A/B experiment and particle field-identity audit in §8.
4. Compute the four power quantities on matched support.
5. Choose the next implementation change from the decision table; do not launch multiple speculative model rewrites.
6. Only after corrected comparisons show what error remains, decide whether an off-axis single-loop FEM isolate, a local mesh/domain check, or SPH reconstruction work is required.

Next pack should include current relevant SPH source and exact invocation, runtime source dump, minimal particle field CSV, raw OnPoint values and representative VT rows, the installed GetDP template, strict parser, mesh/solution identity, and the four-power table. Include the missing Docs 48/52–55 and thermal regression evidence only if asking for those items to be independently closed.

## 11. Format references and important limitations

Official references checked during this review:

- GetDP manual, Jacobian types: https://getdp.info/doc/texinfo/getdp.html#Types-for-Jacobian
- Gmsh manual, postprocessing commands/data ordering: https://gmsh.info/doc/texinfo/#Post-processing-scripting-commands

Gmsh vector-triangle values are arranged by time step, node and component. The current parser's two 9-value blocks are structurally compatible with two vector snapshots, but treating them as real/imaginary requires the actual GetDP complex-output contract. No raw VT row was supplied, so this review does not declare that layout wrong. Verify it with direct OnPoint data instead of introducing another guessed component swap.

GetDP distinguishes axisymmetric Jacobian variants and their field transformations. This does not justify arbitrary manual r/2*pi corrections. Inspect the exact installed template/version and validate physical output through independent A/B/flux checks.

The root cause of the remaining 0.84/0.694 discrepancy cannot be uniquely identified from summary JSONs and comparison code alone. The findings above identify confirmed reporting/comparison defects and a small falsifiable experiment that can separate the remaining causes.

## Appendix A — Reproduce the independent reference

The following standalone Python requires NumPy. It computes a prescribed filament source in free space, not a coupled conducting FEM solution. The elliptic-integral cross-check used in this review additionally used SciPy.

```python
import numpy as np
from numpy.polynomial.legendre import leggauss

MU4PI = 1e-7
CENTERS = -.01 + (np.arange(7) + .5) * .23 / 7

def ab(r, z, n=1024, a=.285, centers=CENTERS):
    r, z = np.broadcast_arrays(np.asarray(r, float), np.asarray(z, float))
    shape = r.shape
    r, z = r.ravel()[:, None], z.ravel()[:, None]
    theta = (np.arange(n) + .5) * 2*np.pi/n
    c, s = np.cos(theta), np.sin(theta)
    dl = 2*np.pi*a/n
    A = np.zeros(len(r)); Br = A.copy(); Bz = A.copy()
    for zc in centers:
        dz = z-zc
        d2 = (r-a*c)**2 + (a*s)**2 + dz**2
        A += MU4PI*np.sum(dl*c/np.sqrt(d2), axis=1)
        Br += MU4PI*np.sum(dl*c*dz/d2**1.5, axis=1)
        Bz += MU4PI*np.sum(dl*(a-r*c)/d2**1.5, axis=1)
    return [v.reshape(shape) for v in (A, Br, Bz)]

def power(nq, nt):
    x, w = leggauss(nq)
    r, z = np.meshgrid((x+1)*.125, (x+1)*.105, indexing='ij')
    A, _, _ = ab(r, z, nt)
    Q = .5*16*(2*np.pi*300000)**2*A*A
    return np.sum(Q*2*np.pi*r*np.outer(w*.125, w*.105))

for q, t in [(24,512), (48,1024), (64,2048)]:
    print(q, t, power(q, t))
for r in [.0001,.05,.10,.15,.20,.24]:
    print(r, .105, *ab(r,.105,2048))
for z in [.02,.19]:
    print(.24, z, *ab(.24,z,2048))
```

## 2. Pitfall fixed before probing

First GetDP attempt used Doc58’s leftover **thin** `work/french_cylinder_axi.msh` with baseline `coil_a=coil_b=0.0025` in `.pro` → effective I scaled by (0.00025/0.0025)² = **1/100**, axis \|Bz\|→1.41e-7. Remeshed baseline (Δr=5 mm) restores axis 1.409e-5 (Doc 57).

**Always** remesh with matched `coil_a/b` before coil-only solves; do not reuse thin meshes with baseline Js area.

SPH probes must pass French coil flags (`--coil-radius=0.285 --coil-num-loops=7 ...`). Bare `--probe-coil-point` defaults to R=0.4 / 8 turns.

1. Immediate post-Biot host audit in `runFrenchReducedAIndOneWayDiagnostic`: for N particles, compare `BCoil` to `accumulateBiotSavartFromMoments` at `Position` **before** edge-flux; dump xyz + Acoil + Bcoil.
2. If pre-edge-flux already fails → debug `applyFilamentBiotSavartToGlassHost` / moments / Position sync.
3. If pre-edge-flux passes and post-edge-flux fails → find the mutating write.
4. Only after stored B matches direct Biot: regenerate SPH↔GetDP B/Q tables and reassess absolute P.

Scripts: `run_doc60_decisive_probe.py`, `french_cylinder_doc60_probes.pro`, `run_doc60_getdp_onpoint.sh` (fix remesh before use).

<a id="62-glass-center-sscanf-float-bug-2026-09-27md"></a>

# File: `62_GLASS_CENTER_SSCANF_FLOAT_BUG_2026-09-27.md`

# Doc 62 — Root cause: `--glass-center=` float/`%lf` parse shifted the melt vs coils

Date: 2026-09-27  
Parents: Doc 60/61 (decisive probe), Doc 57/58 (0.84 / thin-coil)  
Fix: `electromagnetic_ophelie_french_reduced_geometry.h` (`--glass-center=` parse)

## 1. Decision

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| Stored `BCoil` mutated after Biot / by edge-flux | **falsified** (Doc 61 audit: ratio 1.0 pre/post) |
| SPH filament evaluator wrong vs independent 7-loop | **falsified** (direct probe + particle Biot OK) |
| GetDP MagDyn / 5 mm section causes the 0.84 gap | **falsified** (Doc 58 + this doc) |
| `--glass-center=x,y,z` with `Real`+`sscanf %lf` under SYCL (`Real=float`) | **root cause** |
| Effect: melt lattice centered at z≈0 while coils stay at French z∈[-0.01,0.22] | **confirmed** |
| CSV `z_m` cosmetic shift (`z0=-0.105`) hid the misalignment | **confirmed** |
| After fix: midplane drive \|B\| SPH/GetDP ≈ **1.01**, same-I `P_sph/P_getdp` ≈ **1.06** | **exercised** |
| Docs 51/57 absolute-P and 0.84 drive narrative on dumps that passed `--glass-center=` | **superseded** (instrumentation bug, not physics) |

Production `--aind=off` unchanged. No js0/skull/`Js=2πI/A` restore.

## 2. Evidence chain

### 2.1 Frame dump (broken CLI)

```text
glass_center=(0,0,-3.4e-11)  half_h=0.105  z0=-0.105
pos_z_world=[-0.095, 0.085]
coil_z=[-0.01, 0.22]
```

- Biot at **world** positions matched stored `BCoil` (mean ratio 1.000).
- Same fields vs Biot at CSV `z_m = z_world+0.105` gave midplane \|B\| ratio ≈ **0.840** (Doc 57 number).
- Independent heating on CSV `(r,z_m)` over-predicted power vs recon (recon saw the weak, misaligned drive).

### 2.2 Bug

```cpp
// BROKEN under SPHINXSYS_USE_SYCL (Real = float):
Real x,y,z;
std::sscanf(arg, "%lf,%lf,%lf", &x, &y, &z);  // %lf expects double*
```

`--glass-height=` correctly set `center.z = half_h`, then `--glass-center=0,0,0.105` **overwrote** it with a corrupted float parse → `center.z≈0`. Stirring CLI already used `double` temps; reduced French path did not.

### 2.3 After fix

```text
glass_center=(0,0,0.105)  z0=0  pos_z_world=[0, 0.20]
```

| Metric | Pre-fix (Doc 51/57 dumps) | Post-fix Doc 62 |
|---|---:|---:|
| Midplane mean \|B\|_SPH/\|B\|_GetDP coil-only | ≈0.840 | **1.013** |
| All-probe mean \|B\| ratio | ≈0.862 | **1.018** |
| `P_sph / P_getdp` (coil-only SPH vs conducting GetDP) | **0.694** | **1.061** |
| Midplane `corr_Q` | 0.966 | **0.984** |
| Full-glass masked `rel_L2_Q_abs` | 0.436 | **0.104** |
| Midplane shape `rel_L2_Q` | 0.170 | **0.118** |
| `ΔP_outer` (SPH vs GetDP probe) | ≈0.013 | **0.006** |
| `P_recon` [W] | 1.478 | **2.260** |
| `P_recon / P0_particles` | ≈0.67 | **0.972** |

Artifacts:

- Fix: `docs/ophelie/reference/...` via geometry header (see §4).
- Dump: `work/doc60_decisive_probe/sph_rz_fixed_center_doc62.csv`
- Compares: `compare_coil_b_doc62.json`, `compare_aziavg_doc62.json`
- Logs: `sph_fixed_center_doc62.log`, `sph_frame_check.log` (broken baseline)

## 3. What this means for prior docs

- **Doc 50** axis coil-only fix (holes + `Js=I/A`) stands.
- **Doc 58** thin-coil null result stands as a control against section size; it was never the 0.84 cause.
- **Doc 57** “(0.84)²≈P gap ⇒ filament vs finite section” — withdraw causality; the 0.84 was misaligned melt/coil from the CLI bug on dumps that passed `--glass-center=`.
- **Doc 51** absolute P≈0.694 and large full-domain Q L2 on those dumps — regenerate before using as gates; shape/shell were partly measuring the same frame error.
- Equal-power remains a valid **operating-point** tool when the paper fixes absorbed power; it is no longer required to hide a ~30% same-I gap from this bug.

Residual ~6% same-I P and ~1–2% B are in the discretization / recon / conducting-feedback band — not a 16% drive scandal.

## 4. Code change

File: `tests/.../electromagnetic_ophelie_french_reduced_geometry.h`

Parse `--glass-center=` into `double` temporaries, then cast to `Real` (same pattern as stirring translations / `--probe-coil-point`).

Diagnostic dump now also prints `glass_center`, `z0`, `pos_z_world` and writes `x_m,y_m,z_world_m` for frame audits.

## 5. Next

1. Regenerate any archival SPH rz CSVs that were produced with `--glass-center=` under SYCL; re-run azi-avg gates used by Batch C.
2. Grep other `sscanf %lf` into `Real` in non-stirring paths (stirring already safe).
3. Optional: assert `|glass_center.z - half_height| < tol` when French coils use absolute `z_min/z_max`, or derive coil z from glass frame only.
4. Defer 1000 s / Picard-on / skull / paper-T until refreshed EM tables are accepted.

## 6. Claim table

| Claim | Status |
|---|---|
| CLI `glass-center` parse bug under float Real | **fixed / exercised** |
| Melt–coil z alignment restored | **exercised** |
| Same-I B≈1 and P≈1 within ~6% on dp=0.02 lattice | **exercised** |
| Literature temperature closed | **not claimed** |

# File: `63_POST_DOC62_EM_BASELINE_REFRESH_2026-09-27.md`

# Doc 63 — Post–Doc62 EM baseline refresh and residual power budget

Date: 2026-09-27  
Parents: Doc 62 (glass-center fix), Doc 60/61 (probe), Doc 51 (superseded dumps)  
Runner: `docs/ophelie/reference/em_rz_getdp/run_doc63_baseline.sh`

## 1. Decision

| Item | Status |
|---|---|
| Regenerate coil-only + one-way SPH rz CSVs with fixed `--glass-center=` | **done** |
| Frame assert `glass_center.z ≈ half_height` in `refreshFrenchReducedCoilStack` | **done** |
| Other `sscanf %lf`→`Real` in reduced path | **already fixed** (stirring/probe used `double`) |
| New same-I gates (dp=0.02 lattice, I=1 A) | **B≈1.01, P≈1.06, shape L2≈0.10** |
| Residual ~6% P mainly GetDP conducting vs no-feedback P0 | **supported** |
| Doc 51/57 dump numbers as current gates | **superseded** |
| Equal-power | Optional paper operating-point protocol; **not** required to hide a drive bug |
| 1000 s / Picard-on / skull / paper T | **still deferred** |

## 2. Hardening

- `validateFrenchReducedGlassCoilFrame()` after coil-stack refresh: FATAL if `|center.z - half_height|` exceeds tol (Doc 62 failure mode).
- Dump continues to print `glass_center / z0 / pos_z_world` (Doc 62).

## 3. Refreshed compares (`work/doc63_baseline/`)

| CSV | Role |
|---|---|
| `sph_rz_coil_only_dp02_doc63.csv` | coil-only recon (also copied to `work/sph_rz_coil_only_dp02_doc63.csv`) |
| `sph_rz_oneway_dp02_doc63.csv` | one-way feedback |

| Gate | Coil-only SPH vs GetDP | One-way SPH vs GetDP |
|---|---:|---:|
| Midplane mean \|B\| (vs GetDP coil-only) | **1.013** | (same Bcoil) |
| `P_sph / P_getdp` (conducting GetDP) | **1.061** | **1.100** |
| Midplane `corr_Q` | **0.984** | **0.983** |
| Midplane shape `rel_L2_Q` | **0.118** | — |
| Full masked `rel_L2_Q_abs` | **0.104** | **0.122** |
| `ΔP_outer` vs GetDP probe | **0.006** | **0.0005** |

## 4. Power residual budget (I=1 A)

| Quantity | W | /P0 |
|---|---:|---:|
| P0 continuum (Doc 60, no feedback) | 2.27539 | 1 |
| P0 on SPH particle support (soft Biot) | ≈2.325 | 1.022 |
| SPH coil-only `P_recon` | **2.25973** | **0.993** |
| GetDP conducting `P_from_J` | **2.12892** | **0.936** |
| SPH one-way total | 2.34162 | 1.029 |

Interpretation (corrected Doc 66/67):

1. Aligned SPH coil-only is **~0.69% below P0_continuum** but **~2.80% below P0_particles_soft**. The earlier “within ~1% of P0_particles” wording was wrong; continuum agreement cancels a +2.17% support/softening difference against a −2.80% reconstruction difference.
2. GetDP conducting sits ~6.4% below P0 — mixes induced feedback, finite domain, FEM discretization and any residual source-definition differences; Doc63 did **not** experimentally decompose that term.
3. Same-I `P_sph/P_getdp≈1.06` is therefore largely **mode mismatch** (no-feedback SPH dump vs conducting FEM), not a midplane shape failure (corr_Q≈0.98, L2≈0.10). Do **not** retune Js to force those two powers equal.
4. One-way feedback raises SPH P by ~3.6% vs coil-only; shell `ΔP_outer` becomes negligible. That does not by itself prove improved overall EM accuracy.

## 5. Supersession note

Treat as historical (wrong melt/coil frame) whenever dumps used `--glass-center=` under SYCL before Doc 62:

- `work/sph_rz_*_doc50.csv` absolute P≈0.694 / B≈0.84 tables in Docs 51/57  
- Any Batch C gate that consumed those CSVs without regeneration  

Use Doc 63 CSVs + `summary.json` / `power_residual.json` going forward.

## 6. Next

1. Thermal/stirring smoke with declared Doc 63 Q (still `--aind=off`) — F02–F07 only if a transient question appears.  
2. Optional: GetDP Flag_coil_only glass Joule (~0) sanity; or equal-power protocol note in production README.  
3. Still defer 1000 s / Picard-on / skull retune / paper contour fitting.

## 4. F02–F07 reading (this smoke only)

| ID | What this run shows | What it does **not** close |

| F07 | Mass and `rho0·V` / `rho·V` monitors present; Mass stable | Energy measure vs evolving volume still not reconciled |

## 5. Stirring vs frame assert

`syncFrenchStirringGlassToFrenchParams` sets `glass_center` from STL bbox then calls `refreshFrenchReducedCoilStack` → `validateFrenchReducedGlassCoilFrame`. For `glass-z.stl` (z∈[0,0.21]) this is `center.z == half_height` by construction.

`refreshMeltExtentFromParticles` updates `half_height` and `center.z` together from `(z_floor, z_top)` and does **not** re-enter `refreshFrenchReducedCoilStack`, so free-surface tracking does not re-trigger the FATAL. Floor remains `center−half_H` from the initial STL sync (z≈0).

## 6. Explicit non-goals (unchanged)

- No 5-rev observation refresh in this doc.
- No 1000 s campaign, Picard-on production, skull δ retune, or paper-contour T fitting.
- Doc 56 numbers remain historical; Doc 64 is the post–Doc63 coupled smoke reference.

## 7. Next (only if needed)

1. Optional: true F02 cadence twin (`csv=screen=20` vs `100`) on a short frozen or short physical window.
2. Optional: 5-rev observation under the same hardened binary (not required to accept Doc 63 EM).
3. Continue deferring long campaigns until a new physical question appears.

# Doc 66 — Post-Doc65 review: accepted progress, remaining defects, and next actions

Date: 2026-09-27  
Input: `gpt_doc65_cadence_and_status_2026-09-27.tar.gz`  
Predecessor review: Doc 60  
Purpose: actionable handoff to Cursor; distinguish engineering acceptance from numerical and literature validation.

## 1. Decision

**Continue development. The major melt/coil frame defect has been identified and the supplied parser fix is correct. Accept F02 as engineering-accepted for the exercised constant-property production path and 6 s horizon. Do not reopen the old 0.84 field-ratio problem as an unresolved fundamental EM limitation.**

The remaining bottlenecks have changed:

1. Attribute the smaller EM discrepancy using matched prescribed-field/conducting modes and common support.
2. Reconcile thermal storage with evolving particle volume and mass.
3. Separately establish whether the reported end-of-run stirring velocities are comparable before making quantitative stirring/mixing claims. The supplied evidence does not connect their difference causally to the EM frame repair.
4. Establish spatial/timestep sensitivity and paper-specific operating conditions before literature claims.

The next scientific measurement should be a **passive-heating integral from GetDP's source-only A field**, compared with its conducting solution on the same mesh/domain. This separates FEM feedback from source/discretization effects without production Picard or a long thermal run. Alongside that measurement, apply the small CLI-validation fixes in §4.

## 2. Evidence scope

The archive contains 13 files:

- Docs 62–65 and the discussion brief;
- Doc63 EM summary and power decomposition JSON;
- Doc64 summary, Doc65 comparison JSON, two energy CSVs and the Doc65 runner;
- the current reduced-geometry header.

It omits the current production stirring source, thermal/gate headers, current EM comparison/export scripts, Doc61 raw probes, original SPH/FEM field dumps, solver logs, and current build/input identities.

This review:

- read all supplied files;
- checked the actual updated geometry/parser code;
- compiled extracted current parser/frame functions using float Real and tested positive/negative inputs;
- independently recomputed the reported power budget and cadence differences;
- inspected every supplied energy CSV row for finite values, row counts, time coverage and residual ranges.

GPU and FEM results are supplied run evidence, not reruns here. Missing current source is not replaced with historical source when certifying new behavior. No production solver files were modified.

| Refreshed J/Q comparison | Reported full masked absolute Q L2≈0.10386, midplane shape L2≈0.11827. Major improvement. | Matched-mode and common-support comparison; spatial sensitivity; full-domain shape evidence. |
